In this regard, Article 158 of the Penal Code was changed on 25 August 2017. With this arrangement, it has been decided that there is no room for investigation in cases where the complaint and complaint against the person is abstract and general or that the issue is not a crime. Of course, in doing so, we also have the right to appeal to decisions regarding freedom of rights that have not been ignored and there is no room for investigations against those who report and complain.
